2026-10-11T18:35:57.2407606Z  	LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY INVOKER SET search_path=pg_catalog AS $$
2026-10-11T18:35:57.2408008Z  	DECLARE previous "round_migration_47667d8ea2894143881d851e56c366ee".workflow_rounds%ROWTYPE; next_number bigint; parent_state text;
2026-10-11T18:35:57.2408091Z  	BEGIN
2026-10-11T18:35:57.2408264Z  	 IF current_setting('transaction_isolation')<>'read committed' THEN
2026-10-11T18:35:57.2408549Z  	  RAISE EXCEPTION USING ERRCODE='40001',MESSAGE='workflow round allocation requires read committed';
2026-10-11T18:35:57.2408632Z  	 END IF;
2026-10-11T18:35:57.2409061Z  	 PERFORM 1 FROM "round_migration_47667d8ea2894143881d851e56c366ee".workflow_definitions WHERE app_id=NEW.app_id AND id=NEW.flow_id FOR UPDATE;
2026-10-11T18:35:57.2409359Z  	 IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION USING ERRCODE='23503',MESSAGE='workflow round definition missing'; END IF;
2026-10-11T18:35:57.2409618Z  	 SELECT * INTO previous FROM "round_migration_47667d8ea2894143881d851e56c366ee".workflow_rounds
2026-10-11T18:35:57.2409933Z  	  WHERE app_id=NEW.app_id AND flow_id=NEW.flow_id AND record_id=NEW.record_id
2026-10-11T18:35:57.2410167Z  	  ORDER BY round_number DESC LIMIT 1;

2026-10-11T18:35:57.2582739Z  	DECLARE original_view uuid; original_version uuid;
2026-10-11T18:35:57.2582948Z  	BEGIN
2026-10-11T18:35:57.2583172Z  	 -- Row locking needs UPDATE on immutable versions, which runtime must not have.
